Theorem 216.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The media variety V defined by convergence analysis constraints admits a stratification into 61 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 398 and intersection form of rank 5.

Proof: By the convergence analysis principle applied to media theory, we decompose the problem into 61 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 398. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(5/n). QED.
